#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Master Curriculum Reorganizer and Deduplicator
=============================================
Cleans ~/Downloads, deduplicates raw PDF archives, removes underscores,
builds deep recursive vault hierarchies, and generates Navigation MOCs.
Uses only the Python standard library.
"""

import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import shutil
import sys


def print_step(title: str):
    print(f"\n{'='*70}\n {title}\n{'='*70}")


def clean_stray_directories(base_dir: Path):
    """Removes stray configuration folders in Downloads."""
    stray_obsidian = base_dir / ".obsidian"
    if stray_obsidian.exists() and stray_obsidian.is_dir():
        shutil.rmtree(stray_obsidian)
        print("  [Cleaned] Removed stray directory: ~/Downloads/.obsidian")


def deduplicate_pdf_archives(base_dir: Path):
    """Removes duplicate and redundant PDF files, renaming into clean English standards."""
    tai_pdf = base_dir / "TAI PDF"
    if tai_pdf.exists():
        print("  [Processing] Deduplicating and renaming 'TAI PDF'...")
        # Remove duplicate download markers like (1), (2)
        for f in tai_pdf.iterdir():
            if f.is_file() and ("(1)" in f.name or "(2)" in f.name):
                f.unlink()
                print(f"    - Deleted duplicate file: {f.name}")

        # Remove redundant French copies if English version exists
        for f in tai_pdf.iterdir():
            if f.is_file() and "version pour étudiants_ex.pdf" in f.name:
                f.unlink()
                print(f"    - Deleted redundant French file: {f.name}")

        # Standardize clean English filenames (no underscores, no French text)
        rename_map = {
            "TP1_TAI version pour étudiants_eng26.pdf": "TP 1. Image Fundamentals and Histograms (Student Guide).pdf",
            "TP1_TAI version pour étudiants_eng_ex.pdf": "TP 1. Image Fundamentals and Histograms (Exercises).pdf",
            "TAI_TP2.pdf": "TP 2. Advanced Spatial Filtering and Image Restoration.pdf",
            "Getreuer_2009_Contours_Pochoirs.pdf": "Getreuer 2009. Contour Stencils for Edge Adaptive Image Interpolation.pdf"
        }

        for old_name, new_name in rename_map.items():
            old_file = tai_pdf / old_name
            if old_file.exists():
                dest = tai_pdf / new_name
                old_file.rename(dest)
                print(f"    [Renamed] {old_name} -> {new_name}")


def standardize_vault_root(base_dir: Path) -> Path:
    """Renames TAI APDS_Vault to TAI APDS Vault (removing underscore)."""
    old_vault = base_dir / "TAI APDS_Vault"
    new_vault = base_dir / "TAI APDS Vault"

    if old_vault.exists() and not new_vault.exists():
        old_vault.rename(new_vault)
        print("  [Renamed Vault] 'TAI APDS_Vault' -> 'TAI APDS Vault' (No underscores)")
        return new_vault
    elif new_vault.exists():
        return new_vault
    return old_vault


def build_recursive_vault_hierarchy(vault_root: Path):
    """Restructures flat chapter folders into a deep recursive hierarchy with subfolders."""
    print("  [Restructuring] Building deep recursive subfolders in vault...")

    # Specification: Chapter -> Subfolder -> List of source file fragments
    structure_map = {
        "01. Introduction to Data Science": {
            "01.1. Theoretical Foundations": [
                "01.1. Data Science Foundations and CRISP-DM Workflow.md",
                "01.2. The Modern Data Ecosystem and Professional Roles.md"
            ],
            "01.2. Computational Environment": [
                "01.3. Setting Up the Professional Python and Data Science Environment.md"
            ],
            "01.3. Practical Tutorials": [
                "01.4. Tutorial 02 - Hospital Patient Cohort Initialization and Environment Audit.md"
            ]
        },
        "02. Python Programming Essentials": {
            "02.1. Runtime and Primitives": [
                "02.1. Python Runtime, Dynamic Typing, and Core Primitives.md",
                "02.10. Code Modularity, Packaging, and Standard Library Systems.md"
            ],
            "02.2. Data Structures and Memory Architecture": [
                "02.2. Python Lists, Sequence Operations, and Internal Memory Architecture.md",
                "02.3. Dictionaries, Sets, Tuples, and High-Performance Collections.md"
            ],
            "02.3. Control Flow and Streaming Systems": [
                "02.4. Control Flow, Conditionals, and Comprehensions Mastery.md",
                "02.5. Exhaustive Guide to Iteration, Iterables, and the Iterator Protocol.md",
                "02.6. Generators, Lazy Evaluation, and Asynchronous Streaming Pipelines.md",
                "02.7. The 1 Million Records Memory Explosion Autopsy and Production Streaming Solutions.md"
            ],
            "02.4. Object-Oriented Design and Error Handling": [
                "02.8. Object-Oriented Programming and Class Design Patterns.md",
                "02.9. Robust Exception Handling and Resource Management.md"
            ],
            "02.5. Practical Tutorials": [
                "02.11. Tutorial 03 - Modular Sales Inventory Processing Pipeline.md",
                "02.12. Tutorial 03 - Student Cohort Generation and Statistical Analysis System.md"
            ]
        },
        "03. Essential Python Libraries": {
            "03.1. Theoretical Foundations": [
                "03.1. Contextual Data Science Computing with NumPy and Pandas.md"
            ],
            "03.2. Practical Tutorials": [
                "03.2. Tutorial 04 - Multi-City Temperature Array Analysis with NumPy.md",
                "03.3. Tutorial 04 - Academic Performance Statistical Analysis with NumPy and Matplotlib.md",
                "03.4. Tutorial 04 - Hospital Department Patient Analytics with Pandas.md",
                "03.5. Tutorial 04 - Longitudinal Sales Forecasting Project.md"
            ]
        },
        "04. Data Cleansing and Preprocessing": {
            "04.1. Cleansing and Transformation Theory": [
                "04.1. Missing Data Mechanisms and Statistical Imputation.md",
                "04.2. Statistical Outlier Detection and Consistency Rectification.md",
                "04.3. Categorical Feature Encoding and Text Normalization.md",
                "04.4. Feature Scaling, Standardization, and Normalization Theory.md"
            ],
            "04.2. Practical Tutorials": [
                "04.5. Tutorial 05 - End-to-End Medical Cohort Preprocessing Pipeline.md",
                "04.6. Tutorial 05 - Titanic Predictive Modeling Pipeline and Classifier Evaluation.md"
            ]
        },
        "05. Exploratory Data Analysis": {
            "05.1. Analytical Principles": [
                "05.1. Exploratory Data Analysis Principles, Distributions, and Correlation Metrics.md"
            ]
        },
        "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)": {
            "06.1. Acquisition and Topology": [
                "06.1. Digital Image Acquisition, Matrix Topologies, and Fixed Thresholding (TP1).md",
                "06.2. Spatial Transformations, ROI Extraction, and Multi-Channel Histograms (TP1).md"
            ],
            "06.2. Spatial Restoration and Smoothing": [
                "06.3. Discrete Noise Models and Spatial Convolution Smoothing (TP2 Part 1).md",
                "06.4. Fast Integral Filtering and Adaptive Spatial Smoothing (TP2 Part 1 Homework).md"
            ],
            "06.3. Non-Linear Edge Preservation": [
                "06.5. Edge-Preserving Non-Linear Smoothing with the Nagao Filter (TP2 Part 2).md"
            ],
            "06.4. Mathematical Morphology": [
                "06.6. Mathematical Morphology, Cellular Counting, and Zhang-Suen Thinning (TP2 Part 3).md"
            ],
            "06.5. Contrast Dynamics and Equalization": [
                "06.7. Contrast Dynamics, Histogram Equalization, and CLAHE (TP2 Part 4).md"
            ],
            "06.6. Single-Frame Super-Resolution": [
                "06.8. Single-Frame Super-Resolution and Getreuer Contour Stencils (TP2 Part 5).md"
            ]
        }
    }

    # Execute file moves into subfolders
    for ch_name, subfolders in structure_map.items():
        ch_dir = vault_root / ch_name
        if not ch_dir.exists():
            continue

        for sub_name, file_list in subfolders.items():
            sub_dir = ch_dir / sub_name
            sub_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

            for target_file in file_list:
                # Find file anywhere inside chapter directory
                matches = list(ch_dir.rglob(target_file))
                if matches:
                    src_file = matches[0]
                    dest_file = sub_dir / target_file
                    if src_file != dest_file:
                        shutil.move(str(src_file), str(dest_file))
                        print(f"    [Organized] {target_file} -> {sub_name}/")


def create_chapter_navigation_mocs(vault_root: Path):
    """Generates index Map of Content (MOC) notes inside each chapter and subfolder."""
    print("  [Generating Navigation MOCs] Creating index hubs...")

    for ch_dir in sorted(vault_root.iterdir()):
        if not ch_dir.is_dir() or ch_dir.name in [".obsidian", "Libraries"]:
            continue

        moc_file = ch_dir / f"{ch_dir.name[:2]}.0. Chapter Overview and Navigation.md"
        sub_items = [d for d in sorted(ch_dir.iterdir()) if d.is_dir() and not d.name.startswith(".")]

        content = [
            f"# {ch_dir.name} - Chapter Overview and Navigation",
            "",
            "## 1. Chapter Structure",
            "```mermaid",
            "mindmap",
            f"  root(({ch_dir.name}))"
        ]

        for s in sub_items:
            content.append(f"    {s.name}")
            for doc in sorted(s.glob("*.md")):
                clean_stem = doc.stem
                content.append(f"      {clean_stem}")

        content.extend([
            "```",
            "",
            "## 2. Navigational Table of Contents",
            ""
        ])

        for s in sub_items:
            content.append(f"### {s.name}")
            for doc in sorted(s.glob("*.md")):
                content.append(f"- [[{doc.name}]]")
            content.append("")

        content.extend([
            "## 3. Prerequisite Library Links",
            "- Connects to foundations in [[00. Library Learning Roadmap]]",
            ""
        ])

        moc_file.write_text("\n".join(content), encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"    [MOC Created] {moc_file.name}")


def main():
    base_dir = Path.home() / "Downloads"
    if not (base_dir / "TAI APDS_Vault").exists() and not (base_dir / "TAI APDS Vault").exists():
        base_dir = Path.cwd().resolve()

    print_step("Phase 1: Cleaning Stray Configurations and Deduplicating PDF Archives")
    clean_stray_directories(base_dir)
    deduplicate_pdf_archives(base_dir)

    print_step("Phase 2: Standardizing Vault Root Directory")
    vault_root = standardize_vault_root(base_dir)

    print_step("Phase 3: Restructuring Deep Recursive Hierarchy")
    build_recursive_vault_hierarchy(vault_root)

    print_step("Phase 4: Generating Chapter Navigation MOCs")
    create_chapter_navigation_mocs(vault_root)

    print_step("Reorganization Completed Successfully!")
    print(f" Your vault is now located at: {vault_root.resolve()}")
    print(" You can now open this folder directly in Obsidian.\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

========================================================================
 FILE: split_vault.py
   Language: python
========================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Master Knowledge Base & APDS Vault Extractor
=============================================
Splits a monolithic `input.md` into a fully organized, deeply hierarchical
Obsidian Knowledge Base vault.

Features:
- State-machine code fence detection (prevents premature splits on Python/Mermaid).
- Dynamic Section 10 table parsing to map each note to its exact folder.
- Fallback heuristic routing based on numeric prefixes (00., 01., 02., etc.).
- Safe cleaning of markdown code-fence wrappers and boundary artifacts.
- Supports both standard modular output and direct TAI APDS Vault integration.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from collections import defaultdict
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sys


class VaultExtractor:
    # Matches file declarations like:
    # ### 01.01. NumPy Foundations - Array Creation, Memory Layout, and Data Types.md
    # ### Exercise 1. Computing 2D Convolution and Boundary Padding.md
    # ### File: path/to/note.md
    FILE_HEADER_PATTERN = re.compile(
        r"^(?:#{1,4}\s*(?:File|Path)\s*[:=\-]\s*[`'\"]?([^\n`'\"]+?)[`'\"]?|###\s+([0-9a-zA-Z_\.\- /]+\.md))\s*$",
        re.IGNORECASE,
    )

    # Matches main section headings like:
    # ## 01. Unified Python Libraries Foundation
    # ## 03. Image Preprocessing, Filtering, and Restoration
    SECTION_HEADER_PATTERN = re.compile(
        r"^##\s+(\d{2}\.[^\n]+)$"
    )

    # Fallback directory map based on the two-digit chapter prefix
    DEFAULT_SECTION_MAP = {
        "00": "00. Navigation",
        "01": "01. Libraries",
        "02": "02. Foundations",
        "03": "03. Preprocessing",
        "04": "04. Segmentation",
        "05": "05. Super Resolution",
        "06": "06. Laboratories",
        "07": "07. Exercises",
        "08": "08. Question Banks",
        "09": "09. Projects",
        "10": "00. Navigation",
    }

    @staticmethod
    def parse_index_table(lines: list[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
        """
        Parses the Master Verification Matrix table in Section 10 to extract
        the exact target directory path for each markdown file.
        Matches table lines like:
        | `01. Libraries/01.01. NumPy Foundations...md` | ... |
        | 02. Foundations/02.1. Physical Image...md | ... |
        """
        table_map: dict[str, str] = {}
        row_regex = re.compile(
            r"\|\s*`?([0-9a-zA-Z_\.\- /]+\.md)`?\s*\|"
        )
        for line in lines:
            match = row_regex.search(line)
            if match:
                full_rel_path = match.group(1).strip()
                filename = Path(full_rel_path).name
                table_map[filename] = full_rel_path
        return table_map

    @staticmethod
    def sanitize_filename(filename: str) -> str:
        """Removes illegal filesystem characters while preserving valid note names."""
        cleaned = filename.strip().replace("\\", "/")
        cleaned = re.sub(r'^[a-zA-Z]:[/]?', '', cleaned).lstrip("/")
        parts = [p.strip() for p in cleaned.split("/") if p.strip() and p.strip() not in (".", "..")]
        target = Path(*parts)
        if target.suffix.lower() not in [".md", ".markdown", ".txt"]:
            target = target.with_suffix(".md")
        return str(target)

    @classmethod
    def peel_outer_fences(cls, lines: list[str]) -> list[str]:
        """Strips accidental outer markdown code wrappers from LLM exports."""
        start = 0
        while start < len(lines) and not lines[start].strip():
            start += 1
        end = len(lines) - 1
        while end >= start and not lines[end].strip():
            end -= 1

        if start > end:
            return []

        first = lines[start].strip()
        last = lines[end].strip()

        # Check if the entire note is wrapped inside an outer ```markdown fence
        match = re.match(r"^(`{3,}|~{3,})(?:markdown|md)?\s*$", first, re.IGNORECASE)
        if match:
            fence = match.group(1)
            char = fence[0]
            closing_pattern = re.compile(r"^" + re.escape(char) + r"{" + str(len(fence)) + r",}\s*$")
            if closing_pattern.match(last):
                lines = lines[start + 1 : end]
            else:
                lines = lines[start + 1 : end + 1]
        else:
            lines = lines[start : end + 1]

        # Strip dangling horizontal rules (---) at the very beginning or end of note
        while lines and (not lines[0].strip() or lines[0].strip() == "---"):
            lines.pop(0)
        while lines and (not lines[-1].strip() or lines[-1].strip() == "---"):
            lines.pop()

        return lines

    @classmethod
    def resolve_destination(
        cls,
        raw_name: str,
        current_section: str | None,
        table_map: dict[str, str],
        mode: str,
    ) -> Path:
        """Resolves the relative destination path for a given note."""
        filename = Path(raw_name).name

        # 1. Lookup in parsed Section 10 table if available
        if filename in table_map:
            rel_path_str = table_map[filename]
        # 2. Check if the header already provided a relative path (e.g. "01. Libraries/note.md")
        elif "/" in raw_name:
            rel_path_str = raw_name
        # 3. Fallback: Determine folder by prefix (e.g. "01.", "02.")
        else:
            prefix_match = re.match(r"^(\d{2})", filename)
            if prefix_match and prefix_match.group(1) in cls.DEFAULT_SECTION_MAP:
                folder = cls.DEFAULT_SECTION_MAP[prefix_match.group(1)]
            elif current_section:
                # Use current section title (e.g. "02. Introduction to Digital Images")
                folder = current_section
            else:
                folder = "Uncategorized"
            rel_path_str = f"{folder}/{filename}"

        dest_path = Path(cls.sanitize_filename(rel_path_str))

        # Adjust routing if integrating directly into existing 'TAI APDS Vault'
        if mode == "tai-vault":
            # Map 01. Libraries -> Libraries (to match existing vault folder)
            parts = list(dest_path.parts)
            if parts and parts[0] == "01. Libraries":
                parts[0] = "Libraries"
            dest_path = Path(*parts)
        elif mode == "flat":
            dest_path = Path(dest_path.name)

        return dest_path

    @classmethod
    def execute(cls, input_file: Path, output_dir: Path, mode: str = "standard") -> int:
        if not input_file.exists():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Source file not found: {input_file}")

        text = input_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        raw_lines = text.splitlines()

        # Pre-pass: Parse Section 10 verification table for absolute path mapping
        table_map = cls.parse_index_table(raw_lines)
        if table_map:
            print(f"[*] Found internal Master Index Table: mapped {len(table_map)} note destinations.")

        current_path: Path | None = None
        current_lines: list[str] = []
        current_section: str | None = None
        files_written = 0
        folder_stats: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)

        # State-machine flags
        code_fence_char: str | None = None
        code_fence_len = 0

        def commit():
            nonlocal current_path, current_lines, files_written
            if current_path is not None and current_lines:
                cleaned = cls.peel_outer_fences(current_lines)
                if cleaned:
                    dest = output_dir / current_path
                    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                    content = "\n".join(cleaned).strip() + "\n"
                    dest.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
                    folder_stats[dest.parent.name] += 1
                    files_written += 1
                    print(f"  [+] Extracted: {dest.relative_to(output_dir)}")
            current_path = None
            current_lines = []

        for line in raw_lines:
            stripped = line.strip()

            # --- Code Fence State Machine ---
            # Handles ``` and ~~~ code blocks to protect inner comments/headers
            fence_match = re.match(r"^(`{3,}|~{3,})", stripped)
            if fence_match:
                marker = fence_match.group(1)
                char = marker[0]
                length = len(marker)
                if code_fence_char is None:
                    code_fence_char = char
                    code_fence_len = length
                elif code_fence_char == char and length >= code_fence_len:
                    code_fence_char = None
                    code_fence_len = 0

            # If inside a code fence, preserve the line as verbatim code
            if code_fence_char is not None:
                if current_path is not None:
                    current_lines.append(line)
                continue

            # --- Section Header Detection ---
            sec_match = cls.SECTION_HEADER_PATTERN.match(line)
            if sec_match:
                current_section = sec_match.group(1).strip()
                # If Section 10 is reached, treat the Verification Matrix as an extra note
                if current_section.startswith("10."):
                    commit()
                    target_name = "00.0. Master Verification Matrix and File Map.md"
                    current_path = cls.resolve_destination(target_name, "00. Navigation", table_map, mode)
                    continue

            # --- File Header Detection ---
            file_match = cls.FILE_HEADER_PATTERN.match(line)
            if file_match:
                commit()
                raw_target = file_match.group(1) or file_match.group(2)
                current_path = cls.resolve_destination(raw_target, current_section, table_map, mode)
                continue

            # --- Buffer Accumulation ---
            if current_path is not None:
                current_lines.append(line)

        # Commit final note
        commit()

        # Print Folder Summary
        print(f"\n{'-'*55}")
        print(f" Extraction Summary by Category:")
        print(f"{'-'*55}")
        for folder, count in sorted(folder_stats.items()):
            print(f"  * {folder:<30} : {count:>2} notes")
        print(f"{'-'*55}")

        return files_written


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Splits merged 'input.md' into a complete Obsidian Vault.",
        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "-i",
        "--input",
        type=str,
        default="input.md",
        help="Path to the input monolithic markdown file (default: input.md)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "-o",
        "--output",
        type=str,
        default="TAI APDS Vault",
        help="Target Vault directory path (default: 'TAI APDS Vault')",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--mode",
        choices=["standard", "tai-vault", "flat"],
        default="tai-vault",
        help=(
            "Routing mode:\n"
            "  'tai-vault' (default): Merges into existing 'TAI APDS Vault' (uses 'Libraries/' folder).\n"
            "  'standard'          : Uses canonical numbers ('00. Navigation', '01. Libraries', etc.).\n"
            "  'flat'              : Writes all notes directly into the root destination without folders."
        ),
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    input_path = Path(args.input).resolve()
    output_dir = Path(args.output).resolve()

    print("\n" + "=" * 65)
    print(" Master Knowledge Base Vault Extractor")
    print("=" * 65)
    print(f" Source File       : {input_path}")
    print(f" Destination Vault : {output_dir}")
    print(f" Mode              : {args.mode}")
    print("=" * 65 + "\n")

    try:
        count = VaultExtractor.execute(input_path, output_dir, mode=args.mode)
        print(f"\n[OK] Success! {count} notes successfully written to: {output_dir}\n")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"\n[!] Error during extraction: {e}\n", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

========================================================================
 FILE: unify_vault.py
   Language: python
========================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
TAI APDS Vault Deduplicator and Chapter Integrator
===================================================
Directly resolves conflicting numbered folders (02. Foundations, 03. Preprocessing, etc.)
by merging their educational content into:
  - '06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/'
  - 'Libraries/'

It deletes the rogue folders, eliminates duplicated files, and rewrites all Obsidian
wikilinks across the vault.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from datetime import datetime
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import shutil
import sys
import zipfile


class VaultCleaner:
    # Source file in rogue folder -> Authoritative destination in TAI / Libraries
    CONSOLIDATION_PLAN = {
        # --- Libraries Cleanup ---
        "Libraries/01.03. NumPy Broadcasting Mechanics and Vectorized Computations.md": (
            "Libraries/04. NumPy Mathematical Engine - Vectorization, ufuncs, and Broadcasting Rules.md"
        ),
        "Libraries/01.11. OpenCV Color Space Transformations and Pixel Math.md": (
            "Libraries/16. OpenCV Core Architecture - Matrix Representation, Channels, and Color Constellations.md"
        ),
        "Libraries/01.13. SciPy Signal and Spatial Operations - 2D Filtering and Morphology.md": (
            "Libraries/19. Spatial Image Restoration - Noise Models, Linear Convolution, and Fast Integral Filters.md"
        ),

        # --- 02. Foundations -> 06.1. Acquisition and Topology ---
        "02. Foundations/02.1. Physical Image Formation and Optical Sensing.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.1. Acquisition and Topology/06.1. Digital Image Acquisition, Matrix Topologies, and Fixed Thresholding (TP1).md"
        ),
        "02. Foundations/02.2. Discretization - Spatial Sampling and Nyquist-Shannon Theorem.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.1. Acquisition and Topology/06.1. Digital Image Acquisition, Matrix Topologies, and Fixed Thresholding (TP1).md"
        ),
        "02. Foundations/02.4. Image Representations - Raster Matrix vs Mathematical Vector Primitives.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.1. Acquisition and Topology/06.2. Spatial Transformations, ROI Extraction, and Multi-Channel Histograms (TP1).md"
        ),

        # --- 03. Preprocessing -> 06.2. Spatial Restoration & 06.3. Non-Linear ---
        "03. Preprocessing/03.3. Linear Smoothing Filters - Box Mean and Separable Gaussian Convolutions.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.2. Spatial Restoration and Smoothing/06.3. Discrete Noise Models and Spatial Convolution Smoothing (TP2 Part 1).md"
        ),
        "03. Preprocessing/03.4. Spatial High-Pass Filtering - Differential Operators and Laplacian Sharpening.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.2. Spatial Restoration and Smoothing/06.3. Discrete Noise Models and Spatial Convolution Smoothing (TP2 Part 1).md"
        ),
        "03. Preprocessing/03.8. Specialized Spectral Operators - Band-Reject Notch Filters and Directional Gabor Filter Banks.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.2. Spatial Restoration and Smoothing/06.4. Fast Integral Filtering and Adaptive Spatial Smoothing (TP2 Part 1 Homework).md"
        ),
        "03. Preprocessing/03.5. Order-Statistic Non-Linear Filtering - Median and Rank-Order Min-Max Operators.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.3. Non-Linear Edge Preservation/06.5. Edge-Preserving Non-Linear Smoothing with the Nagao Filter (TP2 Part 2).md"
        ),
        "03. Preprocessing/03.6. Structural Edge-Preserving Smoothing - The Nagao Adaptive Multi-Sector Filter.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.3. Non-Linear Edge Preservation/06.5. Edge-Preserving Non-Linear Smoothing with the Nagao Filter (TP2 Part 2).md"
        ),
        "03. Preprocessing/03.9. Non-Linear Boundary-Preserving Filtering - The Joint Spatial-Radiometric Bilateral Filter.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.3. Non-Linear Edge Preservation/06.5. Edge-Preserving Non-Linear Smoothing with the Nagao Filter (TP2 Part 2).md"
        ),
        "03. Preprocessing/03.10. Partial Differential Equations in Restoration - Perona-Malik Anisotropic Diffusion.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.3. Non-Linear Edge Preservation/06.5. Edge-Preserving Non-Linear Smoothing with the Nagao Filter (TP2 Part 2).md"
        ),
        "03. Preprocessing/03.11. Feature Space Clustering Segmentation - Mean-Shift and 2SP-DBSCAN.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.3. Non-Linear Edge Preservation/06.5. Edge-Preserving Non-Linear Smoothing with the Nagao Filter (TP2 Part 2).md"
        ),
        "03. Preprocessing/03.16. Deep Learning Denoising Architectures - From Patch MLPs to Residual DnCNN and Uformer.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.3. Non-Linear Edge Preservation/06.5. Edge-Preserving Non-Linear Smoothing with the Nagao Filter (TP2 Part 2).md"
        ),

        # --- 04. Segmentation -> 06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.7. Segmentation ---
        "04. Segmentation/04.1. Formal Segmentation Axioms and Homogeneity Predicates.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.7. Segmentation and Feature Extraction/06.9. Image Segmentation Axioms, Thresholding, and Connected Components.md"
        ),
        "04. Segmentation/04.3. Local Adaptive Thresholding - Niblack and Dynamic Range-Constrained Sauvola Algorithms.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.7. Segmentation and Feature Extraction/06.9. Image Segmentation Axioms, Thresholding, and Connected Components.md"
        ),
        "04. Segmentation/04.5. Morphological Watershed Segmentation and Topological Distance Transforms.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.7. Segmentation and Feature Extraction/06.9. Image Segmentation Axioms, Thresholding, and Connected Components.md"
        ),
        "04. Segmentation/04.6. Connected Component Analysis - Two-Pass Labeling and Equivalence Trees.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.7. Segmentation and Feature Extraction/06.9. Image Segmentation Axioms, Thresholding, and Connected Components.md"
        ),

        # --- 05. Super Resolution -> 06.6. Single-Frame Super-Resolution ---
        "05. Super Resolution/05.2. Directional Reconstruction - Contour Stencils and Total Variation Minimization.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.6. Single-Frame Super-Resolution/06.8. Single-Frame Super-Resolution and Getreuer Contour Stencils (TP2 Part 5).md"
        ),
        "05. Super Resolution/05.4. Document Image Enhancement - Bi-ESRGAN Dual Path Architecture.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.6. Single-Frame Super-Resolution/06.8. Single-Frame Super-Resolution and Getreuer Contour Stencils (TP2 Part 5).md"
        ),

        # --- Laboratories, Exercises, Projects -> 06.8. Practical Exercises ---
        "06. Laboratories/06.3. Laboratory 3 - Algorithmic Engineering of the Nagao Edge-Preserving Filter.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.8. Practical Exercises and Solutions/06.10. Worked Image Processing Exercises and End-to-End Solutions.md"
        ),
        "07. Exercises/07.1. Exercise 1 - Complete Calculation of 2D Convolution and Boundary Padding Modes.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.8. Practical Exercises and Solutions/06.10. Worked Image Processing Exercises and End-to-End Solutions.md"
        ),
        "07. Exercises/07.3. Exercise 3 - Analytical Sub-Pixel Reconstruction via Bilinear Interpolation.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.8. Practical Exercises and Solutions/06.10. Worked Image Processing Exercises and End-to-End Solutions.md"
        ),
        "09. Projects/09.1. Applied Project - End-to-End Document Cleaning and Text Extraction Pipeline.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.8. Practical Exercises and Solutions/06.10. Worked Image Processing Exercises and End-to-End Solutions.md"
        ),

        # --- 00. Navigation -> Curriculum MOC & Roadmap ---
        "00. Navigation/00.0. Master Verification Matrix and File Map.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.0. Advanced Image Processing Index and Curriculum MOC.md"
        ),
        "00. Navigation/00.1. Master Index and Knowledge Graph MOC.md": (
            "06. Advanced Image Processing (TAI)/06.0. Advanced Image Processing Index and Curriculum MOC.md"
        ),
        "00. Navigation/00.2. Course Roadmap and Unified Learning Path.md": (
            "00. Master Curriculum Roadmap.md"
        ),
    }

    ROGUE_FOLDERS = [
        "00. Navigation",
        "02. Foundations",
        "03. Preprocessing",
        "04. Segmentation",
        "05. Super Resolution",
        "06. Laboratories",
        "07. Exercises",
        "09. Projects",
    ]

    def __init__(self, vault_dir: Path, dry_run: bool = False):
        self.vault_dir = vault_dir.resolve()
        self.dry_run = dry_run
        self.link_map: dict[str, str] = {}
        for src, dest in self.CONSOLIDATION_PLAN.items():
            self.link_map[Path(src).stem] = Path(dest).stem

    def create_backup(self) -> Path:
        timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
        backup_zip = self.vault_dir.parent / f"backup_before_unify_{timestamp}.zip"
        print(f"[*] Creating zip backup of vault at: {backup_zip.name}")
        with zipfile.ZipFile(backup_zip, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
            for root, _, files in os.walk(self.vault_dir):
                for f in files:
                    full_p = Path(root) / f
                    zf.write(full_p, full_p.relative_to(self.vault_dir))
        print(f"[OK] Backup created.\n")
        return backup_zip

    @staticmethod
    def extract_unique_body(src_text: str) -> str:
        """Strips duplicate top-level title and clean lines from source note."""
        lines = src_text.splitlines()
        # Skip leading title if present
        while lines and (lines[0].startswith("# ") or not lines[0].strip() or lines[0].strip() == "---"):
            lines.pop(0)
        return "\n".join(lines).strip()

    def merge_and_relocate(self):
        print("[*] Merging fragmented content into authoritative chapter files...")
        merged_count = 0

        for src_rel, dest_rel in self.CONSOLIDATION_PLAN.items():
            src_file = self.vault_dir / src_rel
            dest_file = self.vault_dir / dest_rel

            if not src_file.exists():
                continue

            src_content = src_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            clean_body = self.extract_unique_body(src_content)

            if not dest_file.exists():
                # Destination does not exist: create it with the source content
                if not self.dry_run:
                    dest_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                    dest_file.write_text(src_content, encoding="utf-8")
                    src_file.unlink()
                print(f"  [Created Target] {dest_rel}")
            else:
                # Destination exists: append source content under an integrated section
                dest_content = dest_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
                # Avoid exact duplicate content injection
                if clean_body[:100].lower() not in dest_content.lower():
                    heading = f"\n\n---\n\n## Integrated Subject Knowledge: {src_file.stem}\n\n"
                    merged = dest_content.rstrip() + heading + clean_body + "\n"
                    if not self.dry_run:
                        dest_file.write_text(merged, encoding="utf-8")
                        src_file.unlink()
                    print(f"  [Merged Content] {src_rel} -> {dest_rel}")
                else:
                    if not self.dry_run:
                        src_file.unlink()
                    print(f"  [Already Merged / Removed] {src_rel}")

            merged_count += 1

        print(f"[OK] Consolidated {merged_count} notes.\n")

    def remove_rogue_folders(self):
        print("[*] Deleting rogue duplicate folders from top level...")
        for folder_name in self.ROGUE_FOLDERS:
            folder_path = self.vault_dir / folder_name
            if folder_path.exists() and folder_path.is_dir():
                if not self.dry_run:
                    shutil.rmtree(folder_path, ignore_errors=True)
                print(f"  [DELETED FOLDER] {folder_name}/")

        print("[OK] Top-level vault cleaned of all duplicate green folders.\n")

    def update_wikilinks(self):
        print("[*] Harmonizing Obsidian wikilinks across all notes...")
        updated = 0
        link_regex = re.compile(r"\[\[([^\|\]]+?)(\|[^\]]+)?\]\]")

        def replace_target(m: re.Match) -> str:
            target = m.group(1).strip()
            display = m.group(2) or ""
            if target in self.link_map:
                new_stem = self.link_map[target]
                return f"[[{new_stem}{display}]]"
            return m.group(0)

        for md in self.vault_dir.rglob("*.md"):
            content = md.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            new_content = link_regex.sub(replace_target, content)
            if new_content != content:
                if not self.dry_run:
                    md.write_text(new_content, encoding="utf-8")
                updated += 1

        print(f"[OK] Updated internal links across {updated} files.\n")


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Clean and unify TAI APDS Vault.")
    parser.add_argument(
        "-v",
        "--vault",
        type=str,
        default="TAI APDS Vault",
        help="Path to TAI APDS Vault (default: 'TAI APDS Vault')",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Simulate actions without changes.")
    parser.add_argument("--no-backup", action="store_true", help="Skip zip backup.")

    args = parser.parse_args()
    vault_path = Path(args.vault).resolve()

    if not vault_path.exists():
        print(f"[!] Directory not found: {vault_path}", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)

    print("\n" + "=" * 65)
    print(" TAI APDS Vault Deep Integrator & Folder Fixer")
    print("=" * 65)
    print(f" Target: {vault_path}")
    print(f" Mode  : {'DRY RUN' if args.dry_run else 'LIVE MERGE & CLEAN'}")
    print("=" * 65 + "\n")

    cleaner = VaultCleaner(vault_path, dry_run=args.dry_run)

    if not args.dry_run and not args.no_backup:
        cleaner.create_backup()

    cleaner.merge_and_relocate()
    cleaner.remove_rogue_folders()
    cleaner.update_wikilinks()

    print("=" * 65)
    print(" [OK] VAULT RESTORATION COMPLETE")
    print("=" * 65 + "\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
